**Session summary (2026-09-05, session 227 — "Yes to alternate lines", team line legs, the Picks screen says when its lines fail, and Mobbin is one route):**

- Same branch, after #484 merged. Matt, in one message: *"Yes to alternate
  lines"*, *"Team line legs, yes build it"*, *"Don't use Udemy, but we should
  use mobbin and that is already properly set up. Test the connection and fix
  whatever is in my MD that says it's not connected"*, and on the flagged
  follow-up *"The Picks screen swallows the odds-view error — not sure what
  this means but fix it"*. Four pieces, one PR.
- **Alternate lines — measured before anything was requested.** `api_call_log`
  for 2026-09-04: the MLB prop event call (13 markets, 13 books) ran **705
  times and averaged 32.0 credits**; the 3-market F5 call at the same books is
  a flat 6, so a market costs ~2 credits per call (13 bookmakers count as two
  regions). Eight alternate markets on every pass is therefore **11,000–14,000
  credits/day**, roughly double the ~7,000 Matt approved. So they ride the
  same event call **at most every `PROP_ALT_REFRESH_MIN` (30) minutes**: the
  daytime passes are hourly already, the evening 10-minute passes carry them
  every third pass — ~370 calls/day, about **6,000–7,500 credits**. The
  first day's `api_call_log` is the number to read back. `config.PROP_ALT_MARKETS`
  lists the eight (hits, total bases, RBIs, runs, walks, strikeouts, hits
  allowed, pitcher walks); WNBA/NBA alternates cost the same per market and
  are a separate decision.
- **Stored under their own market key, never in a model's.** Every
  model-facing read (`scorer._latest_dk_prop_row`,
  `paper_tracker._closing_dk_odds`) takes the newest DraftKings row for
  (game, player, market) with `LIMIT 1`; a second line under `batter_hits`
  would make that row an arbitrary one of two. So `batter_hits_alternate`
  rows are one per (player, line) under that key, the parser keys alternate
  rows by line, an alternate that duplicates the book's standard line is
  dropped, and DraftKings' `batter_home_runs_alternate` keeps its 0.5→HR
  remap while its 1.5 line is now kept instead of thrown away. A 422 with
  alternates in the request **retries without them first**, before the
  DK-only retry, so a key the API rejects can never cost the models their
  standard lines. The gate answers False and warns when its own check fails —
  a broken gate that spent every pass would double the budget silently.
- **The view had to change too.** `v_latest_prop_odds_all_books` returned one
  newest row per (game, market, player, book) — right for a standard market,
  whose line MOVES during the day (678 of 2,092 batter_hits keys changed line
  between passes on 09-04; a per-line key would keep the morning's 0.5 beside
  the evening's 1.5), wrong for an alternate market, where one pass writes
  2+, 3+ and 4+ together. Migration `alternate_prop_lines_view` (applied):
  standard keys keep the one newest row from the same top-1 probe; an
  alternate key returns every row of the newest pass by equality on all five
  index columns. Measured after applying: 9,035 rows for 9,035 keys on the
  157-game date (unchanged for every existing reader), `batter_hits` read
  278 ms (was 310). The app reads the market and its alternate key together
  and folds them (`mobile/src/lib/propLines.ts`); the paged read orders and
  keys by line.
- **Found on the way, NOT fixed, flagged:** 1,546 (game, market, player,
  book) keys since 08-29 carry TWO rows at one `snapshot_at`, DraftKings
  included (163). They are **doubleheaders**: both games of DET@CLE on 09-04
  share `MLB_2026-09-04_DET_CLE`, so both games' props land under one
  game_id and "the newest row" for Jose Ramirez is whichever batch wrote last.
  A game_id that cannot tell game 1 from game 2 is the bug; it predates this
  session and touches the odds ingestor, the scorer and settlement. In
  `docs/followups.md`.
- **Team line legs.** The Teams board's pill was the one control on the Stats
  tab that still left the app. It now opens the same "Add to betslip?" sheet,
  and adds a GAME line leg — the team's moneyline, its spread at the board's
  number, or the game total's Over — through the same store, re-price and
  per-book pricing as a prop line. `LineLegSpec` is a union (`kind: 'game'`;
  stored prop specs have no `kind` and still parse); a total's key leaves the
  team out (Over 8.5 from either row is one bet); labels use the picks' idiom
  ("LAD ML", "WSH +1.5", "SEA vs OAK Over 7.0"); `isGameLine` is true so the
  correlation guard refuses a line leg beside a model game leg on the same
  game. `TeamLineQuote` now carries every book's row at its number for the
  sheet's list; `AddLineSheet` takes a `LineSheetInput` built by
  `propLineSheetInput` / `teamLineSheetInput` and no longer knows which board
  it serves. The pill's arrow-out glyph is gone and its hint says what a tap
  does.
- **The Picks screen says when its lines fail.** `fetchPicksForDate` reports
  each enrichment read that fails (the DK latest-odds view, the line shop,
  the prop line shop) through a callback instead of dropping it on the floor;
  `useTodayPicks` returns `partial` (deduped "what — why", the four look-ahead
  cards included) and `PicksHomeScreen` shows a tappable "Couldn’t load
  today’s lines — …. Picks are unaffected. Retry" banner. Until now an
  unreachable odds view meant empty pills and silence, which is why the
  09-04 timeouts were only ever seen on the Stats board.
- **Mobbin is one route.** `mcp__Mobbin__search_screens` answered with three
  real screens in one call — connected. What said otherwise was the repo's
  `.mcp.json`, which declared a second `mobbin` server that sits
  unauthenticated in every remote session; the harness listed it as
  "requires authentication" and the reply repeated that while the connector
  was fine. Removed; the agent's tool list, the skill, `agents_contract.md`
  and `UX_REVIEW.md` now name the connector as the only route, with the
  reason.
- **Verified.** `tests/test_prop_alternate_lines.py` (18) — parser keys,
  the HR remap, the duplicate drop, the 1.5-only hitter's 0.5 alternate, the
  422 fallback order, the gate's four states, the view's two branches, the
  scorer and settlement still filtering on one exact market — each watched
  to fail under a mutation (key back to (market, player); 422 fallback
  removed; gate failing open; dedupe removed). Full suite 2,451 passed.
  `verify_line_legs` (game legs: pricing at the number, mirrored spreads,
  totals keyed to the game, the correlation guard, the sheet input),
  `verify_stats_odds` (the fold, the ruler finding an alternate line),
  `verify_row_cap`, `verify_betslip`, `verify_preferred_book`,
  `verify_book_links` pass; `verify_stats_pill`'s one failure is the
  pre-existing BookMark check, identical on master. tsc error set: 60 = 60,
  identical to master (all TS2352 casts in queries.ts, none in touched
  files). `ux_scan` on the eleven touched files: 22 findings vs 24 on master,
  **0 blockers vs 2** — the two clear-search buttons got their labels.
  UX review (frontend-ux-designer, Mobbin connected — six references pulled):
  Ship with fixes, no blockers, five Should-fixes and four Considers, **all
  taken**: the totals label is home-first with one decimal on a whole number
  so it is byte-identical to `pick_label` ("LAD vs WSH Over 8.0"); the
  partial banner names a look-ahead failure for what it is ("the upcoming
  UFC card") and says "Today's picks are unaffected"; it is one sentence
  with one reason ("Couldn't load today's lines, the line shop and the prop
  line shop — …") rather than three copies of the Postgres message, three
  lines at most, hidden while reloading so Retry answers at once, with the
  action in an accessibility hint; `radii.sm` for its literal; the sheet
  warns before a second game line on one game ("Your betslip already has
  LAD ML on this game. A parlay takes one game line per game."); the Teams
  board bounces back to the betslip like Players; VoiceOver hears "total
  over 8.5", not "o8.5". Declined: none. Left on record by the review: the
  board offers only the Over side of a total; the betslip's combo warning
  says "game-line legs"; the FAV/DOG tag on an Over/Under leg is meaningless
  (all pre-existing).
- **Not done, on record:** the doubleheader collision above; `prune_odds`
  keeps one row per (game, market, player) per tier, so alternate-line
  history thins to one line per alternate key on old dates (nothing reads it
  yet); alternates can be up to 30 minutes older than the standard line in
  the evening (the same staleness every line has by day); WNBA/NBA alternates
  are Matt's call.

**Session summary (2026-09-04, session 226 — three complaints after the betslip flow shipped: dashes at game time, BetMGM not opening, and "take me to the App Store"):**

- Same branch, an hour after #483. Matt: *"Some players are not showing a
  betting line which doesn't make sense everyone should have a line by this
  point"*, *"I tried to place a parlay with mgm but it didn't open my mgm app
  like it does for DK, we need to test this for all books we have lines for"*,
  *"no lines from fan duel which doesn't make sense"*, and then with the Novig
  App Store page: *"If I don't have the Sportsbook for one of them it should
  take me to the App Store to download it."* Each measured before touching
  anything.
- **The dashes, three causes.** (1) Of 313 hitters priced anywhere today, 23
  are DraftKings **1.5-only** and 12 have no DraftKings line — at "1+" all 35
  showed a dash, because the cell only ever showed the ruler's line (§5, a
  different line is a different bet). (2) Games that had STARTED (6:11pm
  first pitch, Matt's message at 6:45pm) hide their lines by design and
  showed the same dash. (3) FanDuel returns only four MLB prop markets
  through the odds feed today — `batter_home_runs`, `batter_stolen_bases`,
  `pitcher_outs`, `pitcher_strikeouts` — 10,487 rows, none of them hits; the
  ingestor asks for all twelve. So "no lines from FanDuel" on a Hits board
  is the source, and the betslip's Open-with row showing FanDuel at 0/2 is
  the honest reading. Fixes: `buildQuoteIndex` falls back to the member's
  book's OWN line when none posts the ruler's (`offLine`), and the pill
  prints that number under the price ("o1.5") and tells VoiceOver it is the
  book's line, not the board's — the sheet then adds "Over 1.5 Hits"; a
  started game's cell says **Started** on both boards instead of a dash.
- **BetMGM.** Three books' betslip links arrive from the feed as TEMPLATES:
  `sports.{state}.betmgm.com`, `{state}.betrivers.com/…coupon={pickType}|…|{wagerAmount}`,
  `sportsbook.caesars.com/us/{state}/bet/betslip`. The app opened them
  verbatim; `{state}` is not a host, so the open failed and the fallback took
  Matt to the web root with no bet on the slip. DraftKings' links carry no
  placeholder, which is why DraftKings worked. New `useBettingState`
  (Settings → **Your state**, a picker of the 27 online-betting
  jurisdictions, stored on device only) and pure `lib/betslipLinks.ts`:
  fill `{state}`, `single`, empty stake; a template that cannot be filled is
  never opened, and the member is told which setting to set. Filled, each is
  the book's own universal link, which iOS routes to the installed app the
  same way it routes DraftKings'.
- **The App Store.** The app cannot tell whether a sportsbook is installed:
  iOS answers `canOpenURL` only for schemes declared in
  `LSApplicationQueriesSchemes`, and the build declares none (so even
  `dksb://` has always fallen through to DraftKings' universal link). Two
  things instead: the fallback chain is now link → scheme → **App Store** →
  site, and the hand-off sheet offers "Don't have the {book} app? Get it on
  the App Store" outright. Every store id is Apple's own, taken from the
  apps.apple.com URL of each listing via search (the store itself is blocked
  from the sandbox): FanDuel 1413721906, BetMGM 1430875409, Caesars
  1413099571, Fanatics 1616738407, BetRivers 1635357259, Hard Rock
  1572525917, Bally Bet 1590852096, Rebet 6468762763; betPARX ships one app
  per state (MD 1662448269, PA 1605805308, NJ 1605805764), resolved from the
  member's state. iOS only — Google Play package names are not verified.
- **Still open, stated:** the device pass. The sandbox cannot open an app,
  so "BetMGM opens with the bet on the slip once the state is set", each
  book's universal link, and BetRivers' `single|id|` coupon values are on
  Matt to tap through. And alternate lines: the competitor prices every
  threshold because it ingests `*_alternate` markets; today's MLB prop pull
  is 795 event calls at 21.2 credits (16,812/day), and adding five batter
  alternates would add roughly 7,000 credits a day (~4% of the 5M plan) —
  a spend decision for Matt (§1b), not made here.
- **Verified:** `verify_book_links.ts` (new) — the seven real link shapes;
  DK/FD need no state; MGM/Caesars/BetRivers fill; a template with no state
  is never opened; unknown placeholders never opened; the chain order; every
  bettable book's store id or its stated reason; the hand-off's store link;
  the Settings row. `verify_stats_odds.ts` — the own-line fallback, the ruler
  line winning over another book's off-line number, the captions and the
  Started cells. tsc error set master's minus one; ux_scan on the touched
  files one BELOW master's (a pre-existing unlabelled close button fixed in
  passing), none new.
- **UX review (frontend-ux-designer): no blockers, six Should-fixes and three
  Considers, all taken.** The missing-state alert no longer falls through to
  the App Store in the same tap (the alert is the whole answer); the
  off-line caption uses the board's idiom — "2+", "At most 1" — in
  semibold, and the sheet says "The board is on 1+ Hits; DraftKings only
  posts 2+"; "Started" became **Live** / **Final** from `gameStatus()`, so a
  1pm game no longer says "started" at 10pm, and a doubleheader team with a
  game still to come gets no label; the store link wraps at large text and
  reads "Get {book} on the App Store", opened through the same
  `tryOpen`/Alert path as the bet button and through the hook so betPARX's
  per-state page appears once the state loads; Missouri added (BetMGM and
  Caesars live there since 2025-12-01); Delaware left out with the reason
  (BetRivers runs on casino subdomains there, so `de.betrivers.com` would
  not resolve); the hand-off sheet's close X labelled; Settings shows an
  unset state in tertiary with "Set" rather than "Change".

**Session summary (2026-09-04, session 225 — "I gave you bad info": the line pill asks to add to the betslip, and the betslip opens at any book):**

- Same branch. Matt, with a competitor's flow beside ours: *"when you click on
  one of the records bet lines, it shouldn't take you directly to the book, it
  should ask you if you want to add to bet slips then bet slip should allow you
  to add to any book."* This reverses session 215's "the pill is the bet link"
  from the same morning; that guard is retired in `verify_stats_pill.ts` with
  the reason, and the new flow is pinned in `verify_line_legs.ts`.
- **What a Stats line becomes: a LINE leg, not a pick** (`lib/lineLegs.ts`).
  The user's own research bet — player, market, the line the board showed,
  the side. No model made it, so it carries no edge and its win probability
  is odds-implied, exactly like a hand-entered custom leg: fair value, never
  a source of edge. It is never written to `picks` and §1c does not apply;
  it re-prices from the latest line every time the slip resolves, because
  the user asked for "the current line", not a locked number. Priced at
  DraftKings when DraftKings posts the line, else at the best bettable book
  that does; `dkPriced` records which, so the betslip's per-book pricing
  does not credit DraftKings with a leg it never posted — the Open-with row
  shows DK at N−1/N and the bet button (`handoffBookFor`) falls through to a
  book that prices every leg, else the one covering most. §6's DK-only rule
  is about what the MODELS decide on; a line leg has no model decision.
- **Persistence:** `hooks/useLineLegs.ts`, a sibling of `useParlaySlip` —
  a pick leg is a stable KEY resolved against today's picks, a line leg has
  no pick to resolve against, so the whole SPEC is stored and re-priced on
  read (`fetchPropLineRows`, one per-game read per leg, plus the game row).
  `useResolvedSlip` resolves both stores: a spec whose read succeeded and
  priced nothing (game started, line pulled) is pruned like a dead pick key;
  a spec whose read failed is held. Line legs follow pick legs in slip order;
  the bar counts both; the betslip removes and clears them through their own
  store.
- **The ask:** `components/AddLineSheet.tsx` — the player, "Over 0.5 Hits",
  the matchup, every bettable book's price for that exact line (best
  starred, DraftKings badged), "Add to betslip" / "Not now", and "Remove from
  betslip" when it is already in. It says in words that the book is chosen
  on the betslip. Reference-only books (ESPN BET, Pinnacle, Bovada) never
  appear: a row here is an invitation to bet.
- **Scope, stated:** the Teams board's pill still opens the book. A team line
  leg is a game-market model (home/away/over at an anchored spread or total,
  `odds` rows rather than prop rows) and is a follow-up; the pill verifier
  pins the current Teams behaviour so the inconsistency is on the record
  rather than accidental. `PlayerStatsScreen`'s Add card stays gated on a
  model pick at the chart's line. Saved parlays file a line leg as a custom
  leg (no game_id), so a restored slip hands it off at DraftKings.
- **Verified:** `verify_line_legs.ts` — DK-posted line priced at DK with the
  other bettable books riding along; reference-only and different-line rows
  left out; a DK-less line priced at the best bettable book with `dkPriced`
  false; no bettable price → no leg; odds-implied probability, zero edge,
  offense-neutral correlation class; stable key and pickId; the betslip
  does not credit DK for a DK-less leg and the button falls through; the
  pill opens the sheet and nothing on the Players board opens a book.
  Watched failing under three mutations (DK credited for an unposted line;
  the betslip ignoring `dkPriced`; the pill opening the book again). tsc
  error set: master's minus one, none in touched files.
- **UX review (frontend-ux-designer): no blockers, eleven Should-fixes and
  four Considers, all taken.** The leg card printed the raw `stats_line` id
  and an unlabelled odds-implied probability (now "Your line" and
  "implied 71%", with the pricing book after the odds when DraftKings did
  not post it); four betslip labels attributed a non-DK price to DraftKings
  (the hold note now says "Priced at DraftKings, except {leg} at {book}",
  the stat reads "Implied", the extras say "DK" only when every leg is
  DK's); the bet button opened a partial slip silently (it now says
  "{book} prices N of M legs", the hand-off row says "Not posted at {book}"
  rather than "add manually"); the header count excluded line legs; a line
  leg whose read failed was counted but invisible (now listed as stale and
  clearable through its own store); saved parlays flattened a line leg into
  a custom leg and credited DraftKings (they keep its game, coverage and
  `dkPriced`, and the saved hand-off falls through to a bettable book that
  covers the slip); the sheet's "choose the book later" sentence sat under
  the list (now a subtitle under the title, which is the proposition
  itself), its rows looked like the picker's tappable cards (one card,
  hairline rows, "Yours" on the member's books), its badge was a fixed box
  around bold text with a hex literal, its X had no role, focus landed on
  the backdrop; the Teams pill was pixel-identical to the Players pill
  while still leaving the app (it carries the arrow-out glyph now); the
  from-betslip banner said "open a player". Also found while fixing: a
  DraftKings-only line leg was being credited to EVERY book by the custom
  leg's book-agnostic rule — custom legs are now identified by the absence
  of `dkPriced`, not by the absence of prices. ux_scan on the touched files:
  42 findings on the branch, 42 on master, none new.

**Session summary (2026-09-04, session 224 — the "What {sport} models look at" card is removed from the Models tab, one day after it shipped):**

- **Matt, with a screenshot of the MLB Models tab: "I wanted you to remove from
  all the models the what our models look for."** Every sport, not just the one
  in the screenshot. Shipped as #478, squash-merged as `57ef608`.
- **What went.** `mobile/src/components/ModelInputsCard.tsx`,
  `mobile/src/lib/modelInputs.ts` (415 lines of per-sport copy) and
  `mobile/scripts/verify_model_inputs.ts` — deleted, not orphaned behind a flag.
  The copy was a hand-maintained DESCRIPTION of the backend feature lists, so
  leaving it in the tree means maintaining prose nobody renders; it is one
  `git show` away if the card ever comes back. `TagChip` stays —
  `BuiltInModelDetailScreen` still uses it at `:390`.
- **The card was load-bearing in two ways nobody had noticed, because it
  rendered unconditionally for every sport.** Removing it opened both paths, and
  both were closed in the same PR:
  - `ListEmptyComponent` had no non-loading branch. Reachable the moment every
    model for a sport is paused or retired — and that takes **no app build**,
    since `isModelPaused` prefers the SERVER store over the bundled list. The
    tab would have gone blank white with no explanation. It now says why.
  - The Built-in tab had no loading state on first paint. `builtInWithStats` is
    derived from the static `BUILTIN_MODEL_IDS` list, not from the fetch, so it
    is non-empty immediately and `ListEmptyComponent` never fires: every row
    painted `EMPTY_STATS` with no spinner. That is exactly the "wall of zeros
    indistinguishable from a failed fetch" the comment three lines above it
    claims to prevent. The zeros have two causes and now read differently —
    spinner while the first fetch is in flight, the settled-bets note after.
- **The generalisable half, and the reason this entry is worth grepping later:
  DELETING THE ONE THING THAT ALWAYS RENDERED IS HOW YOU FIND OUT WHAT WAS
  NEVER HANDLED.** An unconditional header is a silent backstop for every
  branch below it. Before removing one, enumerate the states of the list it
  sits on — loading, empty, populated-but-zero — and check which of them had
  no rendering of their own. Both bugs here pre-dated the card and were
  invisible only because it stood in front of them.
- **UX review (standing rule, §1b) ran with live Mobbin references. Verdict:
  ship with fixes.** Two of its three should-fixes are the bullets above. Three
  dispositions worth recording:
  - **Declined, premise inverted:** it argued the removal pushed model rows
    below the fold by promoting `EmptyState` to the top of the scroll container.
    `EmptyState` is ~190pt tall (`paddingVertical: spacing.xxl * 2`) before and
    after; the card sat ABOVE it, so the rows move UP ~70pt. The underlying
    observation — that block is very tall for a list header — stands and is
    open with Matt.
  - **Declined, pre-existing:** neither Models `FlatList` wires `RefreshControl`
    though `useSettledPicksSincePaperStart` returns `refresh`. Real, unrelated
    to this diff, and a follow-up.
  - **Raised, not coded:** `MODEL_TOP_FEATURES` (`mobile/src/lib/markets.ts`)
    covers **8 of 43 listed models**, so with the card gone six of eight sports
    (NBA, NHL, UFC, GOLF, NCAAF, NFL) explain their methodology NOWHERE in the
    app. Measured, not estimated. Open with Matt: drop the explanation entirely,
    or fill out the per-model list on the detail screen.
  - One review item was stale — it read the working tree mid-commit and warned
    of a tree where `ModelsScreen` still imported a deleted file. Both commits
    staged all four files together.
- **Verified.** `npx tsc --noEmit` — 27 errors, all 27 in `src/lib/queries.ts`
  (untouched, pre-existing `GenericStringError` casts), **0 in touched files**,
  and the error SET byte-identical before and after the follow-up commit.
  `node mobile/scripts/ux_scan.mts --changed` — 0 blockers, 1 should-fix (the
  `fontSize: 10` literal on the Custom tab's `statCaption`, also on
  `origin/master`), **0 new findings in touched files**. `package.json`
  untouched, so this is OTA-eligible and shipped as OTA run #60.
- **Deliberately not done.** The `RefreshControl` gap, the `fontSize: 10`
  literal, and the `MODEL_TOP_FEATURES` coverage gap — all flagged in #478's
  body, none widened into this PR. No `Updated-By:` trailer: this is UI, not a
  model update (no threshold, pause, retrain or feature-list change).

**Session summary (2026-09-04, session 223 — "pulling some lines but not all of them": every response is capped at 1,000 rows, and the Stats board asked for 20,000):**

- Same branch. Matt at 4:59pm, DraftKings selected, MLB 1+ hits, "Playing
  today": Harper priced at −161, Betts / Perez / Santana / Springer / Yelich
  a dash. Measured before touching anything:
  - DraftKings had a 0.5 hits line stored for Betts since 03:17 ET and for
    Perez since 01:17 ET, 14–16 snapshots each — not missing, not stale.
    Springer and Yelich are DraftKings 1.5 only (a different bet, §5 of
    best_line: correctly a dash at 1+). Santana has no DraftKings row.
  - The board's own code, run on today's real rows (`buildQuoteIndex` +
    `quoteForRow` under tsx), prices Betts −250 and Perez −264. The logic is
    right; the app did not have those rows.
  - The Stats read is `.eq(game_date).eq(market).limit(20000)`; the hits
    market is **1,912 rows** today. **Supabase caps every PostgREST response
    at max-rows, 1,000 on this project** (no `pgrst.db_max_rows` override on
    `authenticator`, so the platform default applies), silently. The rows
    that fit were the games the skip-scan view emits first — ATL_PHI is 5th
    of 15 in `idx_games_date` order, TOR_KC 11th, WSH_LAD 14th — which is
    exactly the priced/blank split on the screenshot. The direct response
    count could not be taken from here (the sandbox and WebFetch both refuse
    supabase.co; the worker's job registry has no HTTP probe type), so the
    evidence is the config, the row count and the pattern, stated as such.
  - The same cap was truncating the Picks screen's all-books prop read
    (14,580 rows on a full day) and its game-line read (~1,200) — since the
    day they were written. `fetchSettledPicks` already knew: "PostgREST can
    cap a response below the requested range (max-rows)".
- **Fix:** `mobile/src/lib/paging.ts` — `fetchAllPages(page)` drains a read
  in 1,000-row `.range()` windows, advancing by what came back and stopping
  only on an empty page (a short page is the cap, not the end). All seven
  date-bounded all-books reads go through it with a deterministic order
  (`game_id, [market,] player_name, bookmaker`), which range paging needs —
  the Stats props, the Teams lines, the Picks screen's two, and the UFC /
  NFL / NCAAF week windows (a week of NCAAF is ~2,300 rows, so that board's
  line shop was truncated too). The pick detail's two per-game reads stay
  plain: a few dozen rows by construction. Pure module, so the verifier can execute it
  without pulling react-native through esbuild.
- **Verified:** `mobile/scripts/verify_row_cap.ts` — a fake server holding
  1,912 rows behind a 1,000 cap returns all 1,912 in order in three calls; a
  700-row cap below the window still drains; an error rejects rather than
  returning a partial slate; the four dated reads are paged/ordered/unlimited
  and the two per-game reads are game-bounded. Watched failing under two
  mutations (short page treated as the end; the Stats read back on
  `.limit(20000)`). tsc error set: master's minus one (the old
  `as unknown as OddsByBookRow[]` cast is gone), none in touched files.
  **UX review (frontend-ux-designer): no blockers, one Should-fix taken.**
  Paging the Picks screen's whole-day prop read would have been 16
  sequential statements per mount, each re-evaluating the full day's view,
  on eight screens that share no cache. Only signal cards render the
  line-shop chips (PickCard.showLines is BET-only; AVOID kept for Signals),
  so `fetchPicksForDate` now reads the picks first and bounds both all-books
  reads to the BET/AVOID picks' games — and for props their markets and
  players — one page each, with the `game_id` filter landing on the view's
  driving `games` scan. Also from the review: `fetchAllPages` takes a key and
  drops the row a mid-drain write repeats at a page seam; an orphaned JSDoc
  and two copy-pasted volume comments fixed. Declined for now, stated in the
  PR: stopping on a short page (kept the empty-page confirm — one extra cheap
  statement buys robustness to a server-side cap change), and holding the
  Stats/Teams LINE column mounted while lines reload (a layout change for a
  separate front-end pass).
- **Not done, flagged:** `useTodayPicks` is fetched independently by eight
  screens with no shared cache — a session of its own. And the second half
  of Matt's message — the line pill should ASK to add to the betslip, and
  the betslip hands off to any book — is a separate piece of work, started
  next.

**Session summary (2026-09-04, session 222 — "still not seeing the lines": the poller's re-seed was a 700 MB table scan every 15 minutes, and the views had to survive it):**

- Same branch and screenshot as session 221, an hour after #479 merged: the
  Stats board still toasting *"Couldn't load today's lines — canceling
  statement due to statement timeout (57014)"*. Session 221's view fix was
  real (91 s → 3.4 s) but measured in isolation; the app's read was still
  3.7 s cold, and it was not running in isolation.
- **What the postgres log said.** In the one minute of Matt's screenshot
  (20:28 UTC) there were **41 statement timeouts** across EVERY view the app
  reads — `v_latest_dk_odds`, both `_all_books` views, `v_live_game_state_latest`,
  the player-window RPC — and the same storm recurs several times a day
  (43 at 22:50, 41 at 23:50, 38 at 14:00, 44 at 16:20). `pg_stat_activity`
  at that moment: two identical worker statements 27 s into a `DataFileRead`
  wait — the pre-game line poller's fingerprint seed.
- **The seed was a Parallel Seq Scan of the whole 842 MB odds table**
  (`explain analyze`: shared read=88,408 pages, 6.1 s alone, 27 s+ contended),
  once per `PREGAME_POLL_RESEED_SEC` (15 min) and again on every deploy — and
  the two copies were two containers overlapping during the #478/#479
  deploys a minute apart. Session 210 had cut it from once per tick to once
  per 15 minutes; the query itself was never the right shape. `SERVICE_ROLE`
  is doing its job: the worker logs `skipping job pregame_poller`.
- **Fix, poller side (`data/ingestors/pregame_line_poller.py`):** the seed is
  KEYED. `last_known_prices(conn, keys)` takes the (game_id, market) pairs the
  API just quoted and does one backward probe of `idx_odds_book_snap` per key
  (`SEED_SQL`: `unnest` + `LATERAL ... ORDER BY snapshot_at DESC LIMIT 1`),
  measured 0.27 ms and ~5 buffers a key — 4,752 keys in 1.3 s, no scan, no
  sort. A re-seed is now an EMPTY map; `poll_once` looks up only quoted keys
  the map does not hold. "Never seen counts as changed" is unchanged: a key
  with no stored row comes back absent. Text order on `snapshot_at` was
  measured equal to the timestamptz order on all 7,348 keys since 09-01
  before the index was relied on for it.
- **Fix, app side (migration `skip_scan_latest_odds_views`, applied):** both
  `_all_books` views drop DISTINCT ON for a recursive skip scan — drive from
  `games`, one index probe per distinct (market, book) or (market, player,
  book) key, one backward probe for the newest pre-game row. Measured as
  `authenticated` on today's 41 games:

  | read | before | after |
  |---|---|---|
  | `v_latest_odds_all_books`, today | 3,739 ms, 98,941 rows, 24 MB sort | 333 ms, 1,180 rows |
  | `v_latest_prop_odds_all_books`, today, every market (Picks) | 4,541 ms, 168,651 rows | 653–1,570 ms, 14,580 rows |
  | same, `market = 'batter_hits'` (Stats) | 768 ms | 310 ms |

  New index `idx_prop_odds_line_snap` (game_id, market, player_name, bookmaker,
  snapshot_at), 337 MB, created CONCURRENTLY; `idx_prop_odds_game` dropped
  (a prefix of it); an intermediate `idx_prop_odds_date_line` created and
  dropped the same day (370 MB for 200 ms on one screen). Same columns, order,
  types, exclusions; `game_date` now from `games` for props too (0 of 685,152
  rows since 08-28 disagree, 0 lack a games row). `get_advisors(security)`:
  nothing new.
- **Tests.** `tests/test_pregame_line_poller.py`: the seed is one probe per
  key and not a table scan, asks only for keys the map does not hold, runs
  no query when nothing is quoted, ignores other books and in-play quotes,
  writes a never-stored key, and the loop's re-seed is an empty map — each
  watched failing under a mutation (map check dropped; DISTINCT ON restored;
  loop re-reading the table). `tests/test_latest_odds_views.py` pins the two
  views' shape and column order to the app's own column lists.
- **Not done, flagged:** the Picks screen still swallows the odds-view error;
  the prop view's market filter is applied to its output (Postgres does not
  push predicates into a recursive CTE), so Stats pays the whole-day walk —
  a per-market RPC would get ~300 ms back and needs an app change; and the
  underlying growth (every book, every pass, ~2,400 odds rows per game per
  day) is what makes any latest-per-key read expensive — a `latest_*`
  current-state table maintained by trigger is the structural fix if this
  recurs.

**Session summary (2026-09-04, session 221 — the Teams board timed out, and so did "today's lines": one view keyed wrong, one function recomputing a season per tap):**

- Branch `claude/player-stats-line-availability-di7o6w`, restarted from master
  because #474 had merged. Matt's screenshot of the Teams board: *"Connection
  error: canceling statement due to statement timeout (57014)"*, plus the toast
  *"Couldn't load today's lines — canceling statement due to statement timeout
  (57014)"*. Two different queries, both measured before anything was touched
  (§1b: never estimate what you can measure).
- **"Today's lines" (both Picks and Stats): `v_latest_odds_all_books` and
  `v_latest_prop_odds_all_books` applied the app's `game_date = today` filter
  OUTSIDE the DISTINCT ON.** Postgres pushes a predicate through DISTINCT ON
  only for key columns, so every open of the screen de-duplicated the entire
  2.7M-row `odds` table and then kept today. `explain analyze`: **91 s** for the
  game view, the prop view did not finish inside 25 s, against an 8 s timeout on
  `authenticated` (3 s on `anon`). Fix: `game_date` leads the DISTINCT ON key
  (and `market` for props, which the Stats board also filters on). Identical
  grouping — game_id determines game_date. Migration
  `push_game_date_into_latest_odds_views`, **applied**, re-measured: 3.4 s and
  1.0 s cold.
- **The Teams board: `team_stats_board('MLB', 2026)` measured 31,669 ms**
  (shared hit=368,782 read=70,757, temp 796). Its `picked` CTE recomputes the
  pre-game closing line for every finished game of the season on every call —
  1,883 games, 88,040 odds rows sorted to disk to keep 3,766. A better index
  condition (`bookmaker = ANY(...)` so `idx_odds_book_snap` applies) was tried
  and measured at 17.6 s: the cost is 63,000 random heap reads of a 1.3 GB
  table, so no per-call rewrite gets a season under 8 s. **The board is now
  computed once and read many times.** Migration `cache_team_stats_board`,
  **applied**: the heavy body renamed `team_stats_board_compute` (worker-only),
  a `team_stats_board_cache` table (RLS on, SELECT-only policy),
  `refresh_team_stats_board(sport, season)` swapping one pair per transaction,
  and a new `team_stats_board()` with the SAME signature reading the cache —
  **1.9 ms**. The app changes nothing.
- **Why a table and not a matview:** the full compute across 6 sports × 3
  seasons is minutes, longer than a session tool can hold a statement, and
  `REFRESH MATERIALIZED VIEW` is all-or-nothing. Per-pair refresh fits any
  window, one bad NCAAF season cannot cost MLB its board, and RLS applies.
- **Populated by hand for all 18 (sport, season) pairs** — every team sport ×
  (2025, 2026, 2027; NHL/NBA/NCAAF label a season by its ENDING year, so the
  autumn season carries next year's label). MLB 2026 took 34 s, everything else
  under 5 s. The ATS identity held on every populated pair (MLB 2026 1883-1883,
  MLB 2025 1769-1769, NFL 2025 284-284, WNBA 2026 223-223, NCAAF 2026 13-13;
  NCAAF 2025 867-847 for the documented FBS-filter reason).
- **Daily refresh:** `data/team_board_cache.py` + `run_pipeline` step
  `refresh-team-board`, registered right after `refresh-outcomes` (so
  yesterday's finals are settled first); a failed pair is reported as -1 and
  fails the step rather than reading as an empty season. The two worker-only
  functions are on the record in `data/anon_readable.RPC_REVOKE`.
  `get_advisors(security)` after both migrations: nothing new.
- **Tests:** `tests/test_team_board_cache.py` (10) — the three column lists
  agree (cache table, refresh INSERT, thin function's RETURNS TABLE), the
  compute and refresh are revoked BY NAME with no grant back, the cache is
  RLS-on and SELECT-only, the step is in both pipeline tables and after
  settle, a -1 pair fails the step, and one failing pair does not stop the
  rest. Every one was watched to fail under a mutation before it shipped.
- **Found and NOT fixed, flagged for Matt:** the Picks screen swallows the
  odds-view error (an empty best-line pill, no toast), which is why only the
  Stats board surfaced the timeout. And the `team_stats_board` "~0.5 s" note
  in `supabase_schema.sql` was true in August against a smaller odds table —
  a per-call query over a growing table is a timeout waiting for a date.

**Session summary (2026-09-04, session 220 — your sportsbooks becomes a SET: the Stats board shows the best of them, and the betslip button follows):**

- Follow-on to session 214 (#472, merged) after Matt reviewed it. Branch
  `claude/stats-pricing-book-selection-o3ulrz`, restarted from master because
  #472 had merged; PR #474.
- **THE BET BUTTON IS THE MEMBER'S OWN BOOK.** Matt, 2026-09-04: *"the parlay
  button should match the competitor screen shot I sent and it should change to
  match the Sportsbook the user selects as their preferred."* `ParlayScreen`
  and `SavedParlaysScreen` pass `usePreferredBook()` into `handoffBookFor` /
  `savedHandoffBookFor` again; session 214 had pinned both to `MODEL_BOOK`.
  **This is the hand-off only.** The slip is still priced and modeled at
  DraftKings (§6), the "Open with" row still ranks every bettable book by
  payout, and the DraftKings fallback stays: a book that cannot price every leg
  never gets the button, because "Bet on FanDuel" must not open a slip FanDuel
  cannot take.
- **The scope statement moved with it.** The setting is no longer "the Stats
  page's" — it now drives the Stats lines AND where the betslip sends you, so
  the picker sheet, the Settings card and the Explainer are retitled **Your
  sportsbook** and say both halves. The two surfaces disagreeing about what the
  setting does is exactly what session 214 was fixing, so the wording had to
  move with the behaviour rather than after it.
- **Both pricing callouts removed on Matt's instruction** — the note under the
  parlay button ("Priced at DraftKings · tap a book in Open with above…"),
  which the button change made wrong anyway, and the two pricing sentences
  session 214's UX review had added to the Picks header tooltip.
- **The fallback asymmetry is now stated rather than implied.** Stats has no
  fallback (a player your book has not priced shows no line); the betslip does
  (an unpriceable slip opens at DraftKings). Both are deliberate and the
  Settings note says so, because a member who reads "no fallback" on one screen
  will not expect one on the other.
- **Matt declined the prop-lock change** proposed from the backlog: *"Every ODDs
  call for the bet lines should update all the lines on the stats tab. It should
  not matter the bet avoid or non status. That's only important for the picks
  and signals tabs. For stats we just need current lines every time and show the
  sports book the user selected."* **Measured, and he is right that Stats is
  already independent:** the board reads `v_latest_prop_odds_all_books` /
  `v_latest_odds_all_books`, never `picks` — 12,064 rows across 13 books and 338
  players for today, newest snapshot 08:17 ET, so a player with no pick still
  gets a line. The poller's churn is a Picks/Signals-tab concern only. The
  backlog item stays open and UNAPPROVED; nobody should ship it from the file.
- **The UX review found a real bug, not just copy.** Both screens read
  `usePreferredBook()` without its `ready` flag, and the hook seeds to
  DraftKings while it resolves AsyncStorage — so a FanDuel member saw a green
  "Bet on DraftKings" for a frame, and **a tap landing in that window handed off
  to the wrong book with nothing to show it had happened.** On the saved-parlay
  list every card flipped at once. Both now gate on `ready`.
- **The fallback is now visible where it fires.** A caption renders only when
  `handoff.book !== preferredBook` — "FanDuel doesn't price every leg — opening
  DraftKings", and on a saved card the pre-upgrade variant, since a slip saved
  before the member chose a book has no links there at all. This is NOT the
  standing callout Matt removed: it is a state, shown only when the app has just
  overridden the member's own choice on a money-moving action, and it is the
  non-colour carrier for a fallback the button otherwise signals by turning
  green (UX_REVIEW §5).
- **The two canonical comments were the ones that had gone stale** —
  `usePreferredBook`'s docstring still read "nothing else reads it" and
  `sportsbookLinks.openBetslip` still said parlays hand off to DK "regardless of
  the user's display preference". This scope has now been reversed twice in
  three days, both times off a comment, so the docstring states both readers and
  the asymmetry between them.
- **Declined from the review, and why:** its Consider on adding "DraftKings
  priced" to `BookLinesRow`'s detail-screen hint. Matt has now removed board
  pricing copy twice; the picker sheet and the Settings note both say it at the
  point of choosing.
- **Then Matt sent the competitor's screenshots and the setting became a SET.**
  Three shots of the same leaderboard — DraftKings only, FanDuel only, and both
  — plus their "Select Your Betting Apps" picker: *"In the screen shots I have
  DK only selected as my preferred sports book. Then Fan Duel, then book DK and
  Fan Duel and it takes the best line in that case."* Cross-reading the two
  9:18 shots confirms the rule: with both selected, Jones reads DK −252 against
  FanDuel's −280, Maye DK −224 against −270, Stafford FanDuel −650 where DK is
  worse. Best price wins the cell and the badge names the winner.
- **`usePreferredBook` → `usePreferredBooks`.** A `BookKey[]`, normalized to
  `BETTABLE_BOOKS` order so the board never reshuffles its badges, stored under
  a new key and MIGRATING the old single-book value on first load. The set is
  never empty: the last checkmark is inert and says why, because an empty set
  blanks the Stats column with nothing on screen to explain it.
- **`statsOdds` shops within the set.** One `bestOf` helper, and the reason it
  can compare American odds directly is written down: they are monotonic in
  payout across the −100/+100 boundary, so the best price is the numeric max
  and no decimal conversion can get it wrong. Ties go to the earlier book.
  **Spreads and totals anchor before they shop** — books hang different numbers
  and the better price on a different line is a different bet (best_line §5), so
  the anchor is the first selected book posting the market and only books on
  that same number compete. Moneylines shop freely.
- **The betslip follows Matt's rule, not a narrowing.** `handoffBookFor` now
  takes the set and picks the best-PAYING covering book; `savedHandoffBookFor`
  picks by order, because a save stores links and not prices so there is no
  honest way to rank them. Either way the "Open with" row still lists EVERY
  bettable book with its coverage — *"give them the option to place on any
  Sportsbook we have odds for and it tells them if the bet is available
  there"* — so the set changes the default and never the options.
- **Both verifier scripts gained real cases, and both were WATCHED FAILING.**
  Reverting `bestOf` to first-book-wins fails 4 of the new `verify_stats_odds`
  checks; stubbing the covering-book search fails 3 in `verify_betslip`,
  including one that was already there. The new cases cover the tie-break, the
  −100/+100 crossing (+125 beats −155), coverage never shrinking as books are
  added, and the losing book's link never riding along.
- **Matt then sent their BETSLIP shot** — "submitted a bet regardless if you
  have one or multiple sports books selected" — and it confirmed the rule the
  betslip already follows: their "Open with" row lists every book with combined
  odds, `N/A` where the book cannot price the combo, `3/3` coverage on EVERY
  tile, and a star on the best. `BetslipBooksRow` already did all of that.
  The one thing theirs does better is the LEGEND: "★ = Best odds" sits on the
  header row beside the stars, where ours buried the same fact in a
  four-sentence paragraph under the tiles. The legend moved up, the paragraph
  became an (i) tooltip, and the tooltip now also states the thing a member
  cannot otherwise deduce — this row is every book, not just their selected
  ones, because the set decides the green button and never where they may
  place.
- **The UX review's Blocker was right, and it was the one thing the scan could
  not see.** With several books selected, NO cell on either Stats board said
  which sportsbook its price came from: `BookMark` returns null because
  `BOOK_GLYPHS` is empty, so the only visual channel left was fill-vs-outline,
  which separates DraftKings from not-DraftKings and nothing else from anything.
  A member on FanDuel + BetMGM read 25 identical pills under a header saying
  "BEST", tapped one, and was ejected into a sportsbook they could not predict.
  VoiceOver had been told the book all along; sighted users had not.
  **Fixed in `BookMark` itself** — it now reads the set and prints the book's
  3-character label when there is more than one, and still draws nothing when
  there is one (where the header names it and a label per row is the header
  repeated 25 times). It reads the hook rather than taking a prop so the choice
  sits beside the glyph map that will one day replace it, and no board threads a
  flag through its row components. `bookLabelShort` caps at three characters so
  BALLY / REBET / PARX cannot push the pill out of a 72pt column.
- **The same `ready` regression the parlay button had, on both boards.**
  `StatsScreen` and `TeamsBoard` ignored the flag, and their pill is a DIRECT
  hand-off — a tap in the seeded-default frame opened DraftKings for a FanDuel
  member. Both now gate the whole odds column on it.
- **`savedHandoffBookFor` short-circuited on DraftKings**, so a saved slip said
  "Bet on DraftKings" while the builder said "Bet on FanDuel" for the same legs
  — two rules behind two identical-looking buttons, and the docstring described
  neither. DK now takes the same coverage test as every other book, and a new
  verifier case (watched failing) pins it.
- **Copy the review caught:** `booksName` joined with "or", producing
  "DraftKings or FanDuel haven't posted Hits lines today" — read literally, that
  says one of them might have. Split into `booksName` (conjunctive, "and") and
  `booksNoneName` ("Neither X nor Y", always singular verb). Settings showed
  "your 4 sportsbooks" under a card headed "Your sportsbooks", answering
  nothing — now "DK · FD · MGM". Select-all's label said "Clear all" for an
  action that keeps DraftKings. The locked last book's checkmark was grey, so
  the one book definitively ON looked the most off — green now, with a lock icon
  on its sub-line. The sheet's footnote is gone and the subtitle halved: three
  explanations of one rule inside an 85%-height sheet is what squeezes the list.
- **Declined, and why:** the review wanted the parlay's DraftKings caption
  unconditional again. Matt removed it deliberately ("both gone"), so it stays a
  STATE — but it now also fires when the button opens a non-DraftKings book at
  all, not only on fallback, since that is the case where "Potential payout"
  40pt above is a DraftKings number and the button is not. With DraftKings
  taking the slip, the common case, nothing renders.
- **Not built, deliberately:** the competitor's DFS tab (PrizePicks, Pick6). We
  carry no odds for those, so the rows would be checkboxes that change nothing.
  Book logos are still absent for the reason `docs/book_logos.md` records.
- **Verified:** `npx tsc --noEmit` — 62 errors, error-set identical to
  `origin/master` (worktree at `545ed33`), 0 in the 13 touched files.
  `node scripts/ux_scan.mts` over the same 15 files — 29 findings / 3 blockers
  on the branch against 30 / 3 on master, no new ones (the saved-parlay bet
  button gained a role and a label). The three blockers are pre-existing
  icon-only Pressables outside the changed regions. `npx tsx scripts/verify_stats_odds.ts` ALL PASS.
- **Corrected in the backlog:** `mlb_live_total_runs` was recorded at 17 settled
  picks with a re-sweep due at ~50. Measured today: **95 settled BETs, all
  priced, +12.99u (+13.7% on flat stakes)**, most recent today. It has cleared
  the gate; the sweep needs a named human under §1b and has not been run.

**Session summary (2026-09-04, session 219 — the equity curve was still on the April window, because a migration in the active list restored it every pass):**

- **Matt asked two things about the Track Record screen: make the "Since" tile
  say 9/1, and confirm the +64.1u is 9/1-or-later. The first was already fixed
  and shipped; the second was not, and the number was wrong.**
- **The "Since 04-14" tile is a stale JS bundle, not a bug.** Session 217
  (#471) replaced `RECORD_START.slice(5)` with `LIVE_RECORD_START_SHORT`; the
  production OTA for it succeeded at 13:08 UTC. An `expo-updates` bundle applies
  on the NEXT cold start, so the screenshot (10:06 ET) is the pre-update build.
  Force-quit and reopen and the tile reads "Sep 1, 2026". No code change needed.
- **The +64.1u was NOT 9/1-or-later. It was MLB from 2026-04-17 to 2026-09-03.**
  Measured in production: `v_public_track_record_daily` summed to 1,184 picks /
  +64.05u over the full April window, while `v_public_track_record` — the same
  screen's hero card, three inches higher — summed to 70 picks / **+12.02u**
  since 9/1. Two windows, one screen, both published to members. The right
  number for MLB since the live date is **43-27, +17.16%, +12.02u**; NCAAF is
  3-3, **-0.31u**; UFC has no settled pick yet.
- **Root cause: it was not forgotten, it was being REVERTED.** Only the first of
  `live_record_start_2026_09_01.sql`'s two `CREATE OR REPLACE VIEW` statements
  was holding. `track_record_reads_graded_matview.sql` is still in
  `data/view_migrations.py`'s active list, still redefined the daily view, and
  guarded itself with `position('mv_scored_pick_outcomes' in pg_get_viewdef(…))
  > 0`. Once the daily view stopped reading the matview that guard read "not
  applied yet" and the ELSE branch restored the 2026-04-14 definition — on every
  refresh pass, silently. Measured before the fix: the guard evaluated to **0**,
  so the revert was pending, not hypothetical.
- **The generalisable lesson, and why it is worth promoting: AN IDEMPOTENCY
  GUARD THAT ASKS "DOES THE VIEW STILL LOOK LIKE MY OUTPUT?" IS A LOCK, NOT A
  GUARD.** It cannot distinguish "never applied" from "deliberately superseded",
  and a migration runner that executes it every pass will re-apply it over the
  newer definition forever. A guard belongs on the PROPERTY the migration
  establishes — here the live-date gate — not on the shape of its own output.
- **Fixed.** The daily view was repaired in production immediately (it now
  reconciles exactly with the per-model view: 70 picks / +12.02u MLB, 6 /
  -0.31u NCAAF). New `data/migrations/live_record_start_views_2026_09_01.sql`
  owns BOTH published views, guarded on `'2026-09-01'`, single-statement and
  registered in `ACTIVE_MIGRATIONS` after the file that used to own the daily
  view. That file's daily branch is deleted, with the reasoning left in place of
  it. `require_price_for_published_units.sql` and
  `units_precision_for_public_record.sql` were checked against the new
  definitions first — both find their patched expressions and skip.
- **A guard that would have caught it, in the place that runs daily.**
  `tracking/system_health.py` gains `published_record_window` (CRIT): the two
  published views are two aggregations of ONE population, so it reconciles them
  per sport on picks AND units and asserts neither publishes a game before
  `config.PAPER_TRADING_START`. The comparison is factored out as
  `_published_record_problems`, which is why it can be tested on the real
  numbers from both sides of the fix.
- **Fixed in passing:** `data/view_migrations.py` read every migration with
  `path.read_text()` and no encoding — the CLAUDE.md §7 trap, and these files
  are full of box-drawing characters, so on the Windows box this repo actually
  runs on the read raises `UnicodeDecodeError` and the runner logs a failure for
  a migration that is perfectly fine. Now `encoding="utf-8"`.
- **Verified:** the migration's per-model body was diffed against the live view
  with `EXCEPT ALL` in both directions — 0 rows either way over 8 rows — and the
  daily body was applied and its totals re-measured. The DO block itself was
  executed against production and took the skip path on both branches. Ten new
  migration tests and seven reconciliation tests, each watched failing against a
  deliberately broken copy (gate moved back to 2026-04-14; the paused-model join
  dropped; the guard changed to a shape guard; the `dk_odds` filter removed; the
  daily branch re-armed in the old file; the migration unregistered; the
  reconciliation loop, the units comparison and the window check each removed).
  Full suite: **2,412 passed**.
- **Deliberately not done:** nothing was changed about WHICH picks the published
  record counts, or about the sweep views — `mv_scored_pick_outcomes` and the
  full-outcome views keep their 2026-04-14 window and their re-graded semantics,
  because a cut cannot be swept on four days of BET-only history (§7).

**Session summary (2026-09-04, session 218 — the SPOT column's second review: a spoken tier that said "TGH", a column that ate the player's name, and "Jr." for every suffixed pitcher):**

- The follow-up review of the SPOT column (#473) landed **after** that PR was
  merged on Matt's instruction, and returned two more Blockers plus five
  Should-fixes. #473 cannot be reused, so the branch was restarted from master
  and this is a fresh PR — the fixes are live-bug fixes, because the OTA had
  already shipped the column.
- **Blocker 1 — I told Matt the accessibility label carried the tier in words.
  It did not.** The label called `matchupTierLabel`, which returns the
  three-letter glyph the cell had just STOPPED printing, so VoiceOver announced
  "TGH spot" and "NEU spot" ("new spot") while sighted users had colour alone.
  New `matchupTierWord` returns Favourable / Tough / Neutral. **An abbreviation
  drawn on screen is a label; spoken, it is noise** — and the failure came from
  moving a string between the two roles without re-reading it.
- **Blocker 2 — the column ate the player's name.** `matchupWrap` and `oddsWrap`
  were `minWidth` with RN's default `flexShrink: 0`, and `rowMain` is the row's
  only shrinkable child: measured at 375pt, the name column went 141pt → 105pt,
  and at Dynamic Type the two right columns grew without bound until the name
  reached zero around fontScale 2. Now min/max/shrink on both. A first attempt
  used a hard width — it pins the header perfectly and then clips the PRICE at
  about fontScale 1.3, which is the one number a user came for.
- **The ERA goes back, reversing my own call and the reviewer's first one.**
  Handedness is un-actionable on a board that never shows the hitter's bat side;
  the tier colour is a 3-bucket step function with cliffs at 4.60 and 3.40
  around a ~4.10 league average, so it separates the tails while the number
  separates the middle where most rows live; and `matchup.text` — the only other
  carrier — now reaches a screen reader and nothing else, so dropping the number
  removed it from the product for every sighted user.
- **`lastName` shipped "Jr." for every suffixed pitcher.** `parts[parts.length - 1]`
  on a real probables feed: "Nestor Cortes Jr." → `Jr.`, "Jose De Leon" → `Leon`.
  Now strips generational suffixes and keeps particles. This one was live.
- **The tier colour moved off the team abbreviation onto the pitcher.**
  `colors.bet`/`avoid` are BET/AVOID semantics, so a green `vs HOU` on a board
  of prices reads as a side — and the hit-rate column 60pt away was already a
  traffic light about something else. Also: "TBD" no longer fires for a NAMED
  starter with no ERA yet; the WNBA detail shortened to fit the column it was
  specced for; an em-dash keeps the two-line rail straight; and the
  `ColumnHeader` doc comment the reviewer caught me deleting is restored.
- **A guard grepped for a constant when the function was importable.** The
  `lastName` check asserted `/SUFFIXES/`, which `SUFFIXES_X` satisfies — the
  FOURTH prefix match this session. Replaced with six executable cases that run
  the real function; two separate breaks now fail it. **Where behaviour can be
  executed, execute it** rather than reading the source for its shape.
- **Two of my own verification runs were wrong before they were right**: a tsc
  baseline built in a worktree with no `node_modules` (932 phantom errors), and
  a branch run invoked from the repo root, which has no tsconfig and printed
  tsc's help text. Both caught by the numbers not making sense.
- Verified: 51 checks in `verify_stats_pill`, 13 breaks each failing it; `tsc`
  identical to master (62); eight verify scripts pass; `ux_scan` on the two
  touched files identical to master; pytest 2,387 passed.

**Session summary (2026-09-04, session 215 — the Stats line pill becomes the bet button, the rows lose their sublines, and the stat groups become tabs):**

- Matt, with a competitor's leaderboard screenshot beside ours: *"I want to
  mirror exactly how they show the draft kings line and its betable link
  directly to that sports book … I also like how their lines are clean. Nothing
  under the player name … For us batting and pitching is floating to nowhere's
  can you have those have the same pattern as players and team."* Branch
  `claude/player-stats-line-availability-di7o6w` (restarted from master after
  #448 merged), PR #471.
- **The pill is now the bet link.** Filled in the book's own colour
  (`bookButtonColors` — DraftKings green for DK, the app tint elsewhere), price
  plus the book's mark, and ONE TAP OPENS THE BOOK. `StatsLineSheet` is deleted:
  it sat between the pill and the sportsbook, which is exactly the step Matt
  asked to remove. Same treatment on the Teams board.
- **Matt's call on where the betslip went**, asked before building: the pill
  opens the book, and "Add to betslip" moves one tap deeper onto the player's
  detail screen. It shows the model's own pick for the selected stat — its label
  and its DK price, no edge and no EV — because a leg is a bet of record (§1c)
  and the pick carries its own line, so the card can never offer a bet at a
  number nobody priced. `fromParlay` is threaded through the navigation, so the
  Betslip → find-a-leg → back round-trip still completes.
- **Nothing under the player name.** The meta line ("avg 1.0 · vs HOU ·
  C. Javier 6.45 ERA (R)") is gone, and so is its style. The per-row caption
  under the price went with it — the headline above the board already says
  "1+ Hits", and repeating it 25 times down a column is noise the competitor
  does not carry either. The line still reaches a screen reader, in the pill's
  own label, precisely because it is no longer drawn.
- **The stat groups became the second level of the same tab bar.** New shared
  `GroupTabs`/`SegmentTabs` now renders Players | Teams, Hit Rates | Averages,
  BATTING | PITCHING, the NFL's four, the Teams board's three, AND
  PlayerStatsScreen's own row — four hand-rolled variants collapsed into one, so
  they cannot drift apart again. Full width and evenly divided rather than
  scrolling: the widest set is the NFL's four at 25% each. The second level
  drops its top hairline, because two rules a few points apart read as a boxed
  strip rather than one bar.
- **The logos could not be sourced, and the four routes are on the record.**
  `docs/book_logos.md`: the agent proxy denies `upload.wikimedia.org` and every
  CDN (`CONNECT tunnel failed, 403`), `WebFetch` returns `EGRESS_BLOCKED` on the
  same host, npm carries no sportsbook icon set (`simple-icons` is 3,457 marks
  and none is a book), and the Railway worker would work but is a deploy cycle
  for a 13pt glyph. So `BookMark` renders the book's short label today and
  carries an empty `BOOK_GLYPHS` registry: dropping the licensed SVGs in is a
  one-file change, and `react-native-svg` (15.12.1) is already a dependency so
  it still ships OTA. **The affiliate kit is the right source, not a scrape** —
  same pixels, with the licence, for a screen that links members to those books.
- **Verified, and every guard watched failing.** New
  `mobile/scripts/verify_stats_pill.ts` — 25 source-derived checks on the three
  decisions, all pass; 14 separate breaks each fail it (pill stops calling
  openBookBetslip, loses the book colour, loses the mark, a meta line returns,
  the rowMeta style returns, either board hand-rolls a group row again, the two
  tab levels stop sharing a component, the second level regains its rule, the
  bar becomes a ScrollView, the mark stops taking the pill foreground or starts
  speaking, the registry is dropped, a stray StatsLineSheet reappears).
  **Four of those guards did not bite on the first pass** and were rewritten:
  they were satisfied by an *import* rather than a call, by a prefix match
  (`<SegmentTabsX` matching `/<SegmentTabs/`), by one of two occurrences, and by
  the word appearing in a doc comment above the declaration. A fifth "pass" was
  a flawed break, not a flawed guard — it renamed the comment instead of the
  const. §1b's rule in its exact form: a guard that dead code can satisfy is not
  a guard.
- The guard also **found real dead code**: PlayerStatsScreen's four group-tab
  styles outlived the JSX that used them.
- `tsc` error set identical to `origin/master` (62, none in touched files).
  `verify_stats_odds`, `verify_stats_board`, `verify_hit_rate`,
  `verify_team_board`, `verify_betslip`, `verify_preferred_book`,
  `verify_player_log`, `verify_retired_models` all pass. `ux_scan` set diff on
  the touched files: **4 findings cleared, 0 new**, and the two new components
  scan clean.
- **The UX designer review said DO NOT SHIP, and it was right on all three
  Blockers.** Every one is fixed:
  1. **A §1c regression I introduced.** The betslip card on the player screen
     called `buildPickIndex` then `idx.get(playerId)` with **no line
     comparison** — so with the chart stepped to 3+, the Add button underneath
     still offered the model's Over 0.5 pick. The sheet it replaced had
     `slipPickFor` for exactly this. Restored, converting the screen's "N+"
     threshold to the market line (`line - 0.5`). `slipPickFor` had become dead
     code in `src/`, which also meant `verify_stats_odds`'s assertion *"the same
     pick does NOT unlock a 2+ board"* was guarding nothing — a passing test
     over no shipping path (§7).
  2. **The Players rows swallowed the pill for VoiceOver.** A `Pressable` is
     accessible by default, so the row collapsed into ONE element: the pill
     stopped being a button and activating anywhere fired the ROW's onPress.
     Pre-existing structure, but the consequence is new — the pill is now the
     ONLY bet link, so a VoiceOver user could not reach a sportsbook at all.
     `accessible={false}` on both rows, with the row's own tap moved onto the
     name block. This also cleared **two of master's six scan blockers**.
  3. **The pill overflowed its fixed 78pt column.** `BALLY` and `REBET` are
     five characters and both are selectable, so `+1200` + the mark ran ~88pt
     into a 78pt box, and at Dynamic Type the *price* took the ellipsis.
- **The mark now renders nothing until a licensed glyph lands.** The reviewer's
  argument is better than mine: the column header already prints `DK`, so a
  per-row "DK" is the header repeated 25 times at 10pt — not a brand mark — and
  it is what pushed the pill past its column. Apple Sports holds the licence and
  still prints the mark ONCE beneath the module rather than per row. `BookMark`
  and `BOOK_GLYPHS` stay; only the text fallback goes.
- **Also taken:** the pill's target was ~38pt after the caption went (down from
  ~45pt) and a mis-tap now ejects the user into a sportsbook — vertical
  `hitSlop` and a `minHeight`; the column sizes to content (`minWidth`) instead
  of pinning a width; **only DraftKings is filled**, because `bookButtonColors`
  returns near-black for the other twelve and 25 solid blocks of it outweighed
  the hit rate the board exists to show; the two tab levels now differ by a 1px
  rule and `textPrimary` rather than only 2pt of type; `GroupTabs`' `second` is
  a prop, because hard-coding it reproduced "floating to nowhere" on the player
  screen, which has no first level; uppercase moved into the style so VoiceOver
  reads words; three dead styles removed from `TeamsBoard`.
- **Left for Matt, flagged not silently decided:** the subline removal costs the
  board the opposing pitcher and his ERA, which on a PROP board is the most
  load-bearing fact after the hit rate. The reviewer recommends restoring it as
  a widened SPOT **column** (honouring "nothing under the player name"
  literally, and matching the NBA leaderboard pattern) rather than as a subline.
  That is a visible density change Matt did not ask for, so it goes to him.
- **The guard set grew to 35 checks, and 24 breaks each fail it.** Two more
  guards had to be tightened after they passed a break: one counted
  `accessible={false}` inside the COMMENT THAT EXPLAINS THE FIX, and one
  required two occurrences of a VoiceOver flag after the branch count dropped to
  one. That is the same failure mode as the first round, three times over now —
  **a guard that matches its own prose is not a guard.**
- **Matt handed the escalated decision back to the designer** — *"Add it as a
  widened spot column or have it be in the player data when you click on a
  record … Whatever the design thinks is best usability and UI."* The designer
  had already answered it inside the review, and against the detail screen: a
  board whose matchup lives one tap deeper *"can no longer be scanned for a bet,
  only for a name."* So the column was built.
- **SPOT is now a real matchup column**, 40pt → 68pt `minWidth`: the opponent
  (`vs HOU`) coloured by tier, over one fact beneath it. `MatchupInfo` gains
  `detail`, computed per grader — the opposing starter and his arm for a batter,
  the lineup's wOBA for a pitcher, the defence's rating for WNBA. The separate
  FAV/TGH/NEU glyph is gone, which is what buys the room for the second line.
- **Handedness, not ERA, is the batter's one fact** — deliberately, and against
  the subline it replaces (`C. Javier 6.45 ERA (R)`). The tier colour is already
  a pure function of ERA (>= 4.60 favourable, <= 3.40 tough), so printing the
  number repeats the colour; handedness is carried nowhere else and decides a
  platoon. Put to the designer to confirm.
- **One deviation from the designer's own spec, flagged rather than shipped
  quietly:** making the tier the opponent's colour drops the FAV/TGH/NEU text,
  so the tier is **colour-only** for a sighted colour-blind user. The tier is
  carried in the cell's `accessibilityLabel`, so VoiceOver is fine — that does
  nothing for someone who can see. The competitor's own screenshot uses a
  coloured LETTER GRADE, i.e. text plus colour. Sent back to the designer to
  rule on.
- Guards grew to 40 checks with 29 breaks. One more guard failed to bite and was
  fixed: it checked for a style DEFINITION rather than its USE, so re-adding the
  glyph's stylesheet entry passed. (The break was wrong too — it added the style
  without rendering it.)
- Final: `tsc` identical to master (62); eleven verify scripts pass; `ux_scan`
  **6 findings and 2 blockers cleared, 0 new**.
- **Not a model update** under §1b — display and hand-off only; nothing here
  reaches a scorer.
**Session summary (2026-09-04, session 214 — the sportsbook picker is the Stats page's setting, not the app's; Picks and Signals are best-line across books and not switchable):**

- Matt, on a screenshot of the Picks header reading *"Prices & bets at FanDuel ·
  DK shown when FD doesn't pr…"* and the picker sheet behind it: *"Remove that
  copy … This only applied to the stats page: let's call out that. They can
  change books for the stat page but the signals and main page. We generate the
  best lines from multiple sportsbooks and user shouldn't be able to change
  that."* Branch `claude/stats-pricing-book-selection-o3ulrz`.
- **What the preference used to do.** `usePreferredBook` set the DISPLAYED
  price on every board. `PickCard` and `PickDetailScreen` resolved the headline
  quote through `displayQuoteForPick(pick, bookRows, preferredBook)` — the
  user's book where it priced the side, the labeled DK fallback where it did
  not — and the ring, the "yours" tag and the parlay hand-off followed it too.
  Six surfaces, one setting, and the Picks header line was the only place that
  said so.
- **What it does now: the Stats page, and nothing else.** Picks, Signals, the
  pick detail, the betslip and saved parlays all price at DraftKings, the book
  the models score and the record is graded against, and every one of them
  still lists each book's line best price first (`BookLinesRow`,
  `AllBooksCard`, `BetslipBooksRow`). That list is the line shopping; it is not
  the member's to switch.
- **The scope is now stated three times, in the three places it can be read.**
  The indicator (Stats only) reads `Stats lines at FanDuel · this page only`;
  the sheet is retitled **Stats Page Sportsbook** with a subtitle and footnote
  that name the page; the Settings card is **Stats page sportsbook**. The
  Explainer's "which sportsbook" section was rewritten to match — it had told
  users the setting changed the price on each pick.
- **Removed, deliberately:** the `SportsbookIndicator` from `PicksHomeScreen`
  (the copy Matt asked to remove), the `fallsBackToModelBook` prop (the Stats
  usages were the only ones left and both passed `false`), the "yours" ring in
  `BookLinesRow`, the "yours" row tag in `AllBooksCard`, and the tap-to-switch
  book chip in the betslip's "Open with" header, which is now a static
  `best payout first` hint. `selectLineChips`'s pinned book is optional and the
  pick boards pass nothing.
- **Not touched, on purpose.** `StatsLineSheet` and `TeamsBoard` still read the
  preference — they are the Stats page. `handoffBookFor` and
  `savedHandoffBookFor` keep their book parameter (both callers now pass
  `MODEL_BOOK`); the "Open with" row is the line-shop surface and may hand a
  book back in. The storage key is unchanged, so a member who had picked
  FanDuel keeps it — on the Stats page.
- **The §6 invariant is untouched and slightly better served.** The models
  already decided on DK only; the headline price a member reads on a pick is
  now the same number the edge, EV and Kelly stake were computed from, rather
  than their own book's price beside an edge measured elsewhere.
- **UX review (the §1b standing rule) found one Blocker and it was real.**
  `SportsbookPickerSheet`'s container `Pressable` had no `accessible={false}`,
  so VoiceOver collapsed the whole sheet into ONE element — the six book rows,
  the Close X and Apply all unreachable. The identical defect was fixed in
  `StatsLineSheet` on 2026-09-03; this sheet never got it, and removing the
  betslip's book chip had just made this sheet the only way to change the
  setting. Also taken: the picker title to sentence case, one-sentence scope
  copy on the sheet and in Settings (the long version lives in the Explainer,
  and the same paragraph was being read twice in two taps), `hitSlop` on the
  indicator row, the DK-user's "this page only" suffix dropped as a scope they
  cannot see the other side of, and the per-row caption moved to the non-DK
  rows where the no-fallback caveat actually bites.
- **The Picks board had lost every word about its pricing.** With the indicator
  removed, nothing on the board said the price is DraftKings or that the chips
  are best-payout-first — the compact card path suppresses the "best first"
  hint. Two sentences went into the header tooltip rather than reinstating a
  header line.
- **A §6 deviation closed in passing.** `PickCard` computed the displayed stake
  pair off the PREFERRED book's price, so a FanDuel user read a Kelly stake
  derived from a number the edge was not measured at. Pinning the quote to
  `MODEL_BOOK` fixes it.
- **Left for Matt (in the PR):** the parlay's green button is now permanently
  "Bet on DraftKings" while the "Open with" row above may star another book as
  the best payout. §6 permits `best_*` for the hand-off, so pointing the button
  at the starred book is available — that is a behaviour change on a
  money-moving screen, so the button keeps DK and now says why underneath.
- **Verified:** `npx tsc --noEmit` in `mobile/` — the 62-error set is
  byte-identical to `origin/master` (worktree at `04b6759`), 0 in the 18 touched
  files. `python -m pytest -q tests/` — 2387 passed. The suite covers the
  DK-only decision path (`test_multi_book_odds.py`, `test_best_line.py`), which
  this change does not reach; it is display and copy only.
  `node mobile/scripts/ux_scan.mts --changed` — 53 findings over the 18 files
  against 56 on `origin/master` for the same files, no new ones, and the six
  remaining blockers are pre-existing icon-only Pressables in three screens
  this change did not add.
